## `AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Purity Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Grain Bulk Moisture/Temperature Monitoring & Metal QC
- **Intro:** Purity Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Grain Bulk Moisture/Temperature Monitoring & Metal QC — Verify Steady-State Process Conditions: Grain flow rate: 5.0 ± 0.1 t/h; ambient temperature: 25 ± 2°C; relative humidity: 40 ± 5% RH; sensor warm-up time: ≥ 30 minutes.
- **Key tolerance:** Grain flow rate: 5.0 ± 0.1 t/h; ambient temperature: 25 ± 2°C; relative humidity: 40 ± 5% RH; sensor warm-up time: ≥ 30 minutes.
- **Standards:** `ISO 712:2009`, `ISO 7971-3:2019`, `ANSI/ASME B89.1.13-2013 Micrometer and caliper calibration`, `IEC 60529:1989`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-0163-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)

## `AGO-0163-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Sorter Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Grain Drying Tower Continuous Process
- **Intro:** Sorter Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Grain Drying Tower Continuous Process — Verify Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Electrical Isolation: Voltage must be 0 V AC between phases and to ground.
